   124	export async function acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, cmdLabel) {
   125	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   126	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   127	  const childrenPath = `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`;
   128	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   129	  const payload = JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId: `sup-${io.pid}` });
   130	
   131	  const tryExclusive = () => {
   132	    // Fast-path an already-present lock (fakes without O_EXCL semantics rely
   133	    // on this); the wx flag below is the REAL exclusivity on a POSIX fs — a
   134	    // competitor that lands between the check and the write throws EEXIST
   135	    // instead of being clobbered.
   136	    if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) return false;
   137	    try {
   138	      io.fs.writeFileSync(lockPath, payload, { flag: 'wx' });
   139	      return true;
   140	    } catch (err) {
   141	      if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') return false;
   142	      throw err;
   143	    }
   144	  };
   145	
   146	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt += 1) {
   147	    if (tryExclusive()) {
   148	      return {
   149	        ok: true,
   150	        release: () => {
   151	          try {
   152	            if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   153	          } catch {
   154	            // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the dead path.
   155	          }
   156	        },
   157	      };
   158	    }
   159	    const existing = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
   160	    const other = existing.ok ? existing.value : null;
   161	    const alive = other && typeof other.pid === 'number' && typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
   162	    const sameProcess = other && other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
   163	    if (!sameProcess && (!other || other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
   164	      io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: another supervisor (pid ${other?.pid ?? 'unknown'} on ${other?.host ?? 'unknown'}, run ${other?.runId ?? '?'}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
   165	      return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   166	    }
   167	    // Provably dead (or our own stale lock): reap its recorded in-flight
   168	    // child groups BEFORE anything else runs, then reclaim.
   169	    if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) {
   170	      const lines = String(io.fs.readFileSync(childrenPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '');
   171	      /** @type {Map<string, any>} */
   172	      const starts = new Map();
   173	      const retired = new Set();
   174	      for (const line of lines) {
   175	        try {
   176	          const rec = JSON.parse(line);
   177	          if (typeof rec?.endedAt === 'string') retired.add(rec.childId);
   178	          else if (typeof rec?.pgid === 'number') starts.set(rec.childId, rec);
   179	        } catch {
   180	          // A torn record is unreapable — skip it.
   181	        }
   182	      }
   183	      // Kill ONLY genuinely in-flight children (start with no retire): a
   184	      // retired child's pgid may have been recycled by an unrelated live
   185	      // process — killing it would be a pid-reuse casualty (H3).
   186	      for (const [childId, rec] of starts) {
   187	        if (retired.has(childId)) continue;
   188	        if (typeof io.processAlive === 'function' && io.processAlive(rec.pgid)) {
   189	          if (typeof io.processKill === 'function') io.processKill(-rec.pgid, 'SIGKILL');
   190	        }
   191	      }
   192	      try {
   193	        io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
   194	      } catch {
   195	        // Best effort.
   196	      }
   197	    }
   198	    io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other?.pid})\n`);
   199	    try {
   200	      io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   201	    } catch {
   202	      // Already gone.
   203	    }
   204	  }
   205	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);

     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
